### person.educator_deduction — This person's educator expense deduction
Status: **DISAGREE**

```json

Second:

```json

Ruling: representation; i1040gi p. 93, Line 11: "If you were an eligible educator in 2025, you can deduct on line 11 up to $300 of qualified expenses you paid in 2025." Same formula as rounds 1 and 2 (helpers renamed): the smaller of the expenses and $300 for an eligible educator and zero otherwise. They differ only in which of the two dispositive questions is read first: the candidate-unknown records are interview records with zero expenses where the project rightly does not ask eligibility (classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5, project $0.00), and the project-unknown records are partials withholding the expenses where the candidate's zero follows from eligibility false (classm.f8889.2.deepseek, classm.f4952.1.deepseek). No value differs. The reply changed: it sums sc.s179_elected (lines 6 and 29 together) here and enters zero on person.s179_listed; its note says the supplied pages do not determine the split. That is so: the log gave i4562 pages 4 and 11 only, not page 5, which holds the line 6 and line 7 instructions, nor Form 4562; sc.f6 and sc.f29 were published by id and type only. Form 4562 line 8 (the sum of lines 6 and 7, s179.line8) is the same on both sides, so no deduction differs; the printed lines 6(c) and 7 do: stage14.asset.listed_and_not (completed, project $500.00, candidate $900.00), classm.eic.3 and classm.eic.3.deepseek (completed, listed property only: $0.00 against $10,918.00), stage14.veh.suv_179 ($0.00 against $31,300.00). The candidate refusals inherit vehicle screens the project applies to the same returns (scveh low_use, s179_bad, screened; classm.g28.2, stage14.veh.refuse_low_use; refused returns): scope on that kind. Second transcription's note: Line 4b is zero (optional methods are screened). 'Required to file Schedule SE' is read as Schedule SE line 4c being $400 or more.

Ruling: scope; i1040gi EIC Worksheet B, Part 1 (Schedule SE line 3 less line 13) and Part 2 (Schedule C line 31 when you do not file Schedule SE), with i1040sse p. 1, Who Must File Schedule SE: "You must file Schedule SE if: The amount on line 4c of Schedule SE is $400 or more". The reply changed: it chooses Schedule SE line 3 less line 13 when line 4c is $400 or more (se owes) and the Schedule C total otherwise, the two parts of the worksheet; where Schedule SE is not required line 13 is zero and line 3 equals the Schedule C total, so it gives the project's amount wherever both evaluate, and no value differs. Its se_screen and rrta declarations now sit only on the Schedule SE helpers it reads, so the interview-record unknowns of round 2 are gone. The project-refuses are its cited screens: person.se_rrta (generated 139, 205, 330, 385), person.se_screened (stage16.cleanup.f8959_line16_se_refused) and the at-risk refusal (stage14.refuse.at_risk); project-unknown records are partials withholding rrta or se_screen of a Schedule C person, which the interview asks (classm.f8889.2.deepseek, classm.clcw.4.deepseek, classm.eic.3.deepseek); the one candidate refusal is its se_screen placement on a refused return (classm.asset.3 partial). The reply copies its person.dc_earned, which is W-2 box 1 plus the self-employment part; the project's line is the same sum without line 1e, so no value differs (the line 1e omission is a defect of dc_earned, not of this line). The reply is again built on sc.line31 rather than sc.result; they differ only for a loss with amounts not at risk, which the project refuses at sc.loss_allowed (stage14.refuse.at_risk, the one project refusal; i1040sc p. 15, Line 32, "Be sure to attach Form 6198"). The reply is the smallest of its line 7, ira.line10 and person.ira_contrib, the project's ira_line12, and now declares the ira_excess, kbh_short and both_kinds refusals the project also makes; no value differs. Candidate refusals and unknowns are inherited placements of its own lines, ruled there: se_screen and rrta (classm.w2.1.deepseek, classm.f5695.2.deepseek on a completed return), plan_screen (classm.dependent.7.deepseek), other forms' refusals reached through its modified AGI (classm.g28.2.deepseek scveh low_use), and ira_excess or ira_age50 asked where the interview does not ask them (classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5). Project refusals are the cited Pub. 590-A refusals (stage16.refuse.ira.both_kinds, stage16.refuse.ira.kbh_short, stage16.refuse.person.ira_over_limit, stage16.refuse.ira.over_comp; p590a p. 35, excess contributions); project-unknown records are partials withholding sav_trad, where the candidate's zero follows from no contribution. The candidate adds the second condition; the project's line serves Form 8606 Part I for nondeductible contributions and a distribution with basis is screened by retirement.special. No value differs. Candidate refusals and unknowns are inherited placements of its own lines, ruled there: se_screen and rrta (classm.w2.1.deepseek, classm.f5695.2.deepseek on a completed return), plan_screen (classm.dependent.7.deepseek), other forms' refusals reached through its modified AGI (classm.g28.2.deepseek scveh low_use), and ira_excess or ira_age50 asked where the interview does not ask them (classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5). Project refusals are the cited Pub. 590-A refusals (stage16.refuse.ira.both_kinds, stage16.refuse.ira.kbh_short, stage16.refuse.person.ira_over_limit, stage16.refuse.ira.over_comp; p590a p. 35, excess contributions); project-unknown records are partials withholding sav_trad, where the candidate's zero follows from no contribution. Ruling: representation; Form 8606 (2025), Part I: with no 2025 distribution, "Enter the amount from line 3 on line 14". The project reads line 14 of the person's own Form 8606 (line 3, lines 1 plus 2, a distribution refused at f8606.dist); the reply copies its line 3 (its nondeductible amount plus person.ira_basis), the same two amounts, and refuses at its ira_dist_gate. No value differs. Candidate refusals and unknowns are inherited placements of its own lines, ruled there: se_screen and rrta (classm.w2.1.deepseek, classm.f5695.2.deepseek on a completed return), plan_screen (classm.dependent.7.deepseek), other forms' refusals reached through its modified AGI (classm.g28.2.deepseek scveh low_use), and ira_excess or ira_age50 asked where the interview does not ask them (classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5). Project refusals are the cited Pub. 590-A refusals (stage16.refuse.ira.both_kinds, stage16.refuse.ira.kbh_short, stage16.refuse.person.ira_over_limit, stage16.refuse.ira.over_comp; p590a p. 35, excess contributions); project-unknown records are partials withholding sav_trad, where the candidate's zero follows from no contribution. The reply changed: the student-TIN test is now person.tin, but without person.itin_applied, which the project accepts (p970 p. 11: "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return"; ruled in round 1); by reading, a student whose ITIN was applied for by the due date and issued gets no credit from the candidate (no such record in the sample). Generated 47 (round 2's candidate-shows-skipped, the taxpayer with no TIN) now agrees. Its edu_over refusal matches the reworded question (BUG-0197). The worksheet arithmetic is the project's aqee definition. Lines 27 to 30 also omit the filers' TIN test (household.all_tin, amb.2025.edu_filer_tin, owner decision D7(a)); no record in the sample isolates it this round. Lines 28 to 30 carry no refusal declaration, so on stage19.refuse.period_over_person the project refuses at edu_over_refused and the candidate shows an amount on a return its own line 27 refuses: scope on that kind.   # ---------------- Excess social security tax withheld (Schedule 3, line 11) ----------------
  - id: person.employers
    printed: "employers"
    label: "Number of employers (Forms W-2) for 2025"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.one}

  - id: person.overcount
    printed: "overcount"
    label: "Number of employers that withheld more than $10,918.20"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: w2
      field: w2.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [w2.over, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.over
    printed: "over"
    label: "If any one employer withheld more than $10,918.20, you can't claim the excess on your return"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.overcount, {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  - id: person.wages
    printed: "wages"
    label: "Total wages (social security wages and tips, Forms W-2 boxes 3 and 7)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.wages}

  - id: person.withheld
    printed: "withheld"
    label: "Total social security tax withheld (Forms W-2, box 4)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box4}

  - id: person.eligible
    printed: "eligible"
    label: "Had more than one employer for 2025 and total wages of more than $176,100, and no one employer withheld more than $10,918.20"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ge: [person.employers, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.wages, {param: ss.base}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.over, {const: false}]}
    note: "The instructions describe the case of more than one employer and total wages above $176,100; both are encoded as conditions together with the rule that no single employer withheld more than $10,918.20."

  - id: person.excess
    printed: "excess"
    label: "The amount withheld in excess of $10,918.20"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.withheld, take: {param: ss.limit}}

  - id: person.credit
    printed: "credit"
    label: "Excess social security tax withheld (figure separately for you and your spouse)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible, {const: true}]}, then: person.excess}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.rrta]

  # ---------------- Totals over Forms W-2 ----------------
  - id: person.box1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of Forms W-2, box 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box1}

  - id: person.box2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of Forms W-2, box 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box2}

  - id: person.medicare5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Medicare wages and tips, total of Forms W-2 box 5"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box5}

  - id: person.medicare6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Medicare tax withheld, total of Forms W-2 box 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box6}

  - id: person.dc_benefits
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependent care benefits (Forms W-2 box 10, not counting amounts reported as wages in box 1)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.dc_benefits}

  - id: person.h_box10_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Forms W-2 with an amount in box 10"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: w2
      field: w2.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [w2.has_box10, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.dc_has_box10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Any Form W-2 shows dependent care benefits in box 10"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.h_box10_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  # ---------------- Schedule C totals ----------------
  - id: person.sc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Schedules C"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.one}

  - id: person.ho_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Schedules C claiming business use of the home"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: sc
      field: sc.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [sc.ho_uses, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.sc_1a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Schedules C with qualified tips or overtime for Schedule 1-A"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: sc
      field: sc.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [sc.tips_ot, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.sc_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net profit or (loss) from Schedule C, line 31 (all businesses combined)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.line31}

  - id: person.positive_profits
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of all net profits from Schedule C, line 31 (don't include any net losses) (Form 7206, line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.profit_pos}

  - id: person.sehi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employed health insurance deduction, total of Forms 7206 line 14"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.f7206_line14}

  - id: person.qbi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified business income or (loss), total of Schedules C"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.qbi}

  - id: person.mq_q4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Depreciable bases of property placed in service in the last 3 months of the tax year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.mq_q4}

  - id: person.mq_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Depreciable bases of property placed in service during the tax year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.mq_total}

  - id: person.cost179
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total cost of section 179 property placed in service (Form 4562, line 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.cost179}

  - id: person.s179_nonlisted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Section 179 elected cost (Form 4562, line 6, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.s179_elected}
    note: "The supplied pages do not determine the split between line 6 (non-listed) and line 7 (listed property from Part V); the Schedule C entry publishes only a combined elected amount (sc.s179_elected), which is used here."

  - id: person.s179_listed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Listed property section 179 elected cost (Form 4562, line 7)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.zero}
    note: "Not determined by the supplied pages or interface: no Schedule C field separates listed-property section 179 amounts; the combined amount is carried on person.s179_nonlisted and zero is entered here."

  - id: person.income179
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Business income for the section 179 limit (Schedule C net profit figured without the section 179 deduction)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.line31_pre179}
    note: "The Form 4562 line 11 instructions are not in the supplied pages; this reads the Schedule C result figured before section 179 for each business."

  # ---------------- Schedule SE ----------------
  - id: person.se_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE, line 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line6}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]

  - id: person.se_line12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employment tax (Schedule SE, line 12)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line12}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]

  - id: person.se_line13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Deduction for one-half of self-employment tax (Schedule SE, line 13)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line13}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]

  - id: person.h_se_line3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE, line 3"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line3}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]

  - id: person.h_se_owes
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Owes self-employment tax (Schedule SE line 4c is $400 or more)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: owes}}

  - id: person.h_se_line4c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE, line 4c"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line4c}}

  - id: person.h_se_net
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE, line 3, minus the deduction on Schedule 1, line 15"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: person.h_se_line3, take: person.se_line13}

  - id: person.eic_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Worksheet B: line 1e (Schedule SE line 3 plus line 4b, minus line 13) if filing Schedule SE; otherwise line 2c (Schedule C, line 31)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_se_owes, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_se_net}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_se_owes, {const: false}]}, then: person.sc_total}
    note: "Line 4b is zero (optional methods are screened). 'Required to file Schedule SE' is read as Schedule SE line 4c being $400 or more."

  # ---------------- Form 2441 earned income ----------------
  - id: person.dc_earned
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income for the credit: Form W-2 box 1 wages, plus Schedule SE line 3 minus the deduction on Schedule 1 line 15"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.box1, person.h_se_net]}
    note: "Form 1040 line 1z is taken per person as Form W-2 box 1 (other line 1 amounts are screened out by scope.other_income; line 1e taxable benefits are a return-level amount). A self-employment loss reduces earned income."

  - id: person.dc_earned_b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income for lines 18 and 19 (not including dependent care benefits shown on line 12)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.dc_earned}
    note: "Benefits on line 12 exclude box 10 amounts reported as wages in box 1, so box 1 wages contain none of the line 12 benefits; the amount equals the credit earned income."

  # ---------------- SEP: Deduction Worksheet for Self-Employed (Pub. 560) ----------------
  - id: person.sep_s1
    printed: "Step 1"
    label: "Enter your net profit from Schedule C (Form 1040), line 31"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.sc_total}

  - id: person.sep_s2
    printed: "Step 2"
    label: "Enter your deduction for self-employment tax from Schedule 1 (Form 1040), line 15"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.se_line13}

  - id: person.sep_s3
    printed: "Step 3"
    label: "Net earnings from self-employment. Subtract step 2 from step 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.sep_s1, take: person.sep_s2}
    note: "Negative net earnings allow no contribution; floored at zero."

  - id: person.h_one_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}

  - id: person.h_hundred_cents
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One hundred cents (100%)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.hundred_cents}

  - id: person.h_rate_cents
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Plan contribution rate, as a number of cents (whole percent)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: person.h_one_cent, count: person.sep_rate}

  - id: person.h_rate_whole
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Rate plus 100 (Rate Worksheet line 2, in hundredths)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_rate_cents, person.h_hundred_cents]}

  - id: person.sep_s5
    printed: "Step 5"
    label: "Multiply step 3 by step 4 (your rate from the Rate Table for Self-Employed)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: person.sep_s3, part: person.h_rate_cents, whole: person.h_rate_whole, places: 6}
    note: "Step 4's table rate is rate ÷ (1 + rate) for a whole-percentage plan rate, shown to six decimals in the Rate Table; computed here as rate ÷ (100 + rate) rounded to six places."

  - id: person.h_comp_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$350,000 annual compensation limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.comp_limit}

  - id: person.sep_s6
    printed: "Step 6"
    label: "Multiply $350,000 by your plan contribution rate (not the reduced rate)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: person.h_comp_limit, part: person.h_rate_cents, whole: person.h_hundred_cents, places: 2}

  - id: person.sep_s7
    printed: "Step 7"
    label: "Enter the smaller of step 5 or step 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_s5, person.sep_s6]}

  - id: person.sep_s8
    printed: "Step 8"
    label: "Contribution dollar limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.sep_dollar_limit}

  - id: person.sep_s21
    printed: "Step 21"
    label: "Maximum deductible contribution (no elective deferrals: skip steps 9 through 20 and enter the smaller of step 7 or step 8)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_s7, person.sep_s8]}
    note: "Elective deferrals to the self-employed plan (SARSEP) are screened by person.plan_screen, so steps 9 to 20 are skipped."

  - id: person.h_sep_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SEP deduction: your actual contribution, not to exceed your maximum deductible contribution"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_contrib, person.sep_s21]}

  # ---------------- SIMPLE IRA (Pub. 560, chapter 3) ----------------
  - id: person.h_simple_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE salary reduction limit for 2025 ($16,500)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.simple_limit}

  - id: person.h_simple_catchup
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE catch-up contribution limit for your age"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: keyed_lookup, table: simple_catchup, keys: [person.simple_age]}

  - id: person.h_simple_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction limit including catch-up contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_simple_base, person.h_simple_catchup]}

  - id: person.h_simple_comp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Compensation: net earnings from self-employment (line 4 of Schedule SE) before subtracting SIMPLE contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.h_se_line4c}
    note: "Line 4 of Schedule SE is read as line 4c (optional methods screened; below $400 there are no net earnings)."

  - id: person.h_simple_def_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction contributions, not more than the limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.simple_deferral, person.h_simple_limit]}

  - id: person.h_simple_deferral_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction contributions, not more than compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_def_cap, person.h_simple_comp]}

  - id: person.h_simple_match3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "3% of compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_simple_comp, rate: {param: ret.match_rate}}

  - id: person.h_simple_match
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Matching contribution: dollar-for-dollar up to 3% of compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_deferral_ok, person.h_simple_match3]}

  - id: person.h_simple_comp_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Compensation taken into account (not more than $350,000)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_comp, person.h_comp_limit]}

  - id: person.h_simple_nonelective
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "2% nonelective contribution"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_simple_comp_capped, rate: {param: ret.nonelective_rate}}

  - id: person.h_simple_employer_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Maximum employer contribution under the plan formula"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.simple_kind, {const: "match"}]}, then: person.h_simple_match}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.simple_kind, {const: "nonelective"}]}, then: person.h_simple_nonelective}

  - id: person.h_simple_employer
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Employer contributions for yourself, not more than the plan formula amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.simple_employer, person.h_simple_employer_max]}

  - id: person.h_simple_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE IRA deduction for yourself"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_simple_deferral_ok, person.h_simple_employer]}

  - id: person.s16
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employed SEP, SIMPLE, and qualified plans (Schedule 1, line 16) for this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "sep"}]}, then: person.h_sep_ded}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}, then: person.h_simple_ded}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "none"}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.plan_screen]

  # ---------------- Educator expenses (Schedule 1, line 11) ----------------
  - id: person.h_educ_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$300 per eligible educator"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: s1.educator_max}

  - id: person.h_educ_amt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified expenses, up to $300"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.educator_expenses, person.h_educ_max]}

  - id: person.educator_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Educator expenses: if you were an eligible educator, up to $300 of qualified expenses you paid in 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible_educator, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_educ_amt}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible_educator, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}

  # ---------------- HSA (Form 8889) ----------------
  - id: person.hsa_forms
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Forms 8889"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.one}

  - id: person.hsa_split
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Forms 8889 where spouses with separate HSAs had family coverage (line 6 instructions)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: f8889
      field: f8889.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.hsa_agreed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Forms 8889 using an agreed (not equal) allocation"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: f8889
      field: f8889.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [f8889.allocation, {const: "agreed"}]}

  - id: person.hsa_step1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 Step 1 refigured family contribution limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.step1}

  - id: person.hsa_share
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Your allocated share of the family contribution limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.share}

  - id: person.hsa_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "HSA deduction (Form 8889, line 13)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line13}

  - id: person.hsa_taxable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Taxable HSA distributions (Form 8889, line 16)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line16}

  - id: person.hsa_addtax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional 20% tax (Form 8889, line 17b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line17b}

  # ---------------- Schedule 1-A tips and overtime worksheets ----------------
  - id: person.tip_employers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of employers on the Qualified Tips From More Than One Employer Worksheet"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.one}

  - id: person.tip_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add lines 1A through 1E, column (d)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.col_d}

  - id: person.tip_reported
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified tips reported by employers on Form W-2, or by you to employers on Form 4070 (column (b))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.amount}
    note: "Read as the total of column (b); the pages do not otherwise define this interface."

  - id: person.ot_employers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of employers on the Qualified Overtime Compensation From More Than One Employer Worksheet"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: otjob, field: otjob.one}

  - id: person.ot_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add the amounts from lines 1A through 1E, column (b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: otjob, field: otjob.col_b}

  # ---------------- Form 8880 ----------------
  - id: person.sav_ineligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The person who made the contribution was born after January 1, 2008, is claimed as a dependent on someone else's 2025 return, or was a student"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_born, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_student, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.h_sav_both
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional and Roth IRA contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_trad, person.sav_roth]}

  - id: person.sav_line1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional and Roth IRA contributions for 2025 (do not include rollover contributions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_sav_both}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: false}]}, then: person.sav_roth}
    refuses: [person.sav_able]

  - id: person.h_sav_w2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elective deferrals shown in box 12 of Forms W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.sav_deferral}

  - id: person.sav_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elective deferrals to a 401(k) or other qualified employer plan, voluntary employee contributions, and 501(c)(18)(D) plan contributions for 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_sav_w2, person.sav_voluntary]}
    refuses: [person.sav_excess]

  # ---------------- IRA Deduction Worksheet (Schedule 1, line 20) ----------------
  - id: person.ira_contrib
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional IRA contributions for 2025 (worksheet line 11)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_trad}
    refuses: [person.ira_excess]

  - id: person.ira_cov
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Covered by a retirement plan at work or through self-employment (a SEP or SIMPLE)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_work_plan, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [person.plan, {const: "none"}]}

  - id: person.h_ira_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing jointly or qualifying surviving spouse"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "qss"}]}

  - id: person.h_ira_joint_cov
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Joint or QSS column of a person covered by a retirement plan"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.h_mfs_apart
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing separately and lived apart from your spouse for all of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [ssb.lived_apart, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.h_ira_line2_status
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 2 amount for your filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2}

  - id: person.h_ira_line2_nc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$246,000 for the person who wasn't covered by a plan (married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2_not_covered}

  - id: person.ira_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet line 2: amount for your filing status ($246,000 for the person who wasn't covered, if married filing jointly)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_line2_nc
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_line2_status
    refuses: [person.h_mfs_apart]
    note: "The $89,000 amount for married filing separately and living apart has no parameter separate from the filing-status table (which gives $10,000 for mfs); that case is refused rather than mis-entered."

  - id: person.ira_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet line 6: subtract line 5 from line 2 (zero if line 5 is not less than line 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.ira_line2, take: ira.line5}

  - id: person.h_ira_lim7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$7,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit}

  - id: person.h_ira_lim8
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$8,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit_50}

  - id: person.h_ira_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$7,000 if under age 50 at the end of 2025; $8,000 if age 50 or older"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_ira_lim8}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_ira_lim7}

  - id: person.h_ira_full_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_narrow}

  - id: person.h_ira_full_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$20,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_joint}

  - id: person.h_ira_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 amount at or above which the full limit applies ($20,000 joint/QSS covered; $10,000 otherwise)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_ira_full_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_ira_full_narrow}

  - id: person.h_ira_p70
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 times 70%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_70}}

  - id: person.h_ira_p80
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 times 80%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_80}}

  - id: person.h_ira_p35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 times 35%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_35}}

  - id: person.h_ira_p40
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 times 40%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_40}}

  - id: person.h_ira_prod
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by the percentage that applies"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p35
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p40
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p70
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_joint_cov, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p80

  - id: person.h_ira_prod_up
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result isn't a multiple of $10, increase it to the next multiple of $10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: round, of: person.h_ira_prod, unit: 1000, rule: up}

  - id: person.h_ira_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.min}

  - id: person.h_ira_reduced
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result is $200 or more, enter the result; if less than $200, enter $200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [person.h_ira_prod_up, person.h_ira_min]}

  - id: person.ira_line7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet line 7 for this person's column"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}
          then: person.zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, ge: [person.ira_line6, person.h_ira_threshold]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.lines2to6, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, lt: [person.ira_line6, person.h_ira_threshold]}
          then: person.h_ira_reduced
    note: "Line 6 'No' (line 5 not less than line 2) is a STOP: none deductible, carried as zero for that column."

  - id: person.h_ira_712
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 7 or line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.ira_line7, ira.line10]}

  - id: person.ira_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet line 12: the smallest of line 7, 10, or 11. This is the most you can deduct"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_712, person.ira_contrib]}
    refuses: [person.ira_excess, ira.kbh_short, ira.both_kinds]

  - id: person.h_ira_1011
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of worksheet line 10 or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [ira.line10, person.ira_contrib]}

  - id: person.ira_nondeductible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 1: subtract worksheet line 12 from the smaller of line 10 or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ira_1011, take: person.ira_deduction}

  - id: person.ira_8606_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 2: your total basis in traditional IRAs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_basis}
    refuses: [person.ira_basis_adjusted]

  - id: person.ira_files_8606
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "File Form 8606: nondeductible contributions for 2025, or distributions from a traditional IRA with a basis more than zero"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_nondeductible, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_8606_line2, {const: 0}]}

  - id: person.ira_dist_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 is required and in 2025 you took a distribution from a traditional IRA or made a Roth IRA conversion (go to line 4)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_files_8606, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.h_8606_line3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 3: add lines 1 and 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_nondeductible, person.ira_8606_line2]}

  - id: person.ira_8606_line14
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 14 (no distribution or conversion: enter the amount from line 3 on line 14)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.h_8606_line3}
    refuses: [person.ira_dist_gate]

  - id: person.ira_ssb
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA deduction when no one is covered by a retirement plan: the smallest of $7,000 ($8,000 if 50 or older), line 10, or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_ssbcap, person.ira_contrib]}
    note: "Used before the social security worksheet; when anyone is covered and contributions were made, Pub. 915 applies (ssb.pub915 screen), so lines 2 to 6 are not needed here and no circular dependence arises."

  - id: person.h_ira_ssbcap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of the contribution limit or worksheet line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_limit, ira.line10]}

  - id: person.h_ira_se1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net earnings from self-employment minus Schedule 1 line 15"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: person.sc_total, take: person.se_line13}

  - id: person.ira_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet line 9: earned income as a self-employed individual, minus deductions on Schedule 1 lines 15 and 16; if zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ira_se1, take: person.s16}
    note: "Net earnings read as Schedule C net profit; businesses where services are not material are screened by ira.se_capital."

  # ---------------- Form 8863, Part III for this person as a student ----------------
  - id: person.edu_kid
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 7 conditions 1 and 2: under 18; or 18 with earned income less than one-half of support; or over 18 and under 24, a full-time student with earned income less than one-half of support; and at least one parent alive"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_born, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_born, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2007, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_half_support, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2007, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2002, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_fulltime, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_half_support, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_parent, {const: true}]}
    note: "Age bands from birth dates: born before Jan 2, 2008 = at least 18; before Jan 2, 2007 = over 18; before Jan 2, 2002 = 24 or older. Condition 3 (not filing jointly) is a return-level test."

  - id: person.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total adjustments (tax-free assistance 2025, tax-free assistance 2026, refunds)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_a2025, person.edu_a2026, person.edu_refunds]}

  - id: person.h_edu_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "American opportunity credit for this student (line 23 No, line 24 Yes, line 25 No, line 26 No; Form 1098-T, EIN and TIN)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_ein, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.h_edu_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lifetime learning credit for this student (go to line 31)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}

  - id: person.h_edu_a27
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses for the AOC, not more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line27}}

  - id: person.h_edu_a28
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line28}}

  - id: person.h_edu_a29
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25%"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line29}}

  - id: person.h_edu_a30
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 30 amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line30}}

  - id: person.h_edu_l31
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses for the lifetime learning credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: ws4_llc}}

  - id: person.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_edu_a27}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.edu_over]

  - id: person.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_edu_a28}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}

  - id: person.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_edu_a29}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}

  - id: person.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_edu_a30}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}

  - id: person.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (lifetime learning credit)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_llc, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_edu_l31}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_llc, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.edu_over]
```
```
